## Minute 0, pass 3

**Input**


**Tool `writeBrain`**

```json
{
  "text": "Strategy brain (v1, from retro captain A04):\n1. Placement phase: first priority is closing a mill if one is open. Second, block any opponent line with two pieces and a free third point. Third, build our own two-in-a-row where the third point is free and the opponent cannot block both threats at once (double threats win).\n2. Favour junction points with 4 neighbours (5, 11, 14, 20) and the middle-ring midpoints; corners (1, 3, 22, 24) have only 2 neighbours and get stuck.\n3. When removing an opposing piece after our mill, remove a piece that is part of their two-in-a-row or that blocks our line; avoid removing pieces already in mills (they are protected unless all are in mills).\n4. Movement phase: keep at least one piece able to shuttle between two points to form a mill repeatedly (a \"running mill\"). Avoid moving a piece out of a formed mill if the opponent can then block the gap.\n5. Watch mobility: if opponent is about to be blocked (no legal moves) they lose. Count free neighbours before each move.\n6. With 3 pieces left, we may jump anywhere; keep a potential mill line open for that.\n7. Draw rules: 40 turns without a mill after placement, or threefold repetition. If ahead in material, avoid repetition and force progress; if behind, repeat to draw.\n8. Before each move, check: (a) can we mill now, (b) must we block, (c) does our move create a double threat."
}
```

-> Your team's brain is saved as version 1 (246 words).

> 2. Favour junction points with 4 neighbours (5, 11, 14, 20) and the middle-ring midpoints; corners (1, 3, 22, 24) have only 2 neighbours and get stuck.

> 5. Flying (3 pieces): before each move check if X can make two threats with one jump. If so keep pieces spread to block; never fly a blocker off a line X needs. At 3 pieces, losing one = loss.
